## Raw High-Contrast Mirror Selfie JSON Prompt

> Portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
(Vertical 9:16 smartphone aspect ratio). A raw, high-contrast mirror selfie captured with an iPhone 17 Pro. NO ARTIFICIAL BOKEH. Everything is SHARP and in focus, from the decorative tree in the foreground to the sofa in the background.

Subject & Identity Locking:

Identity: Strictly preserve the exact face and unique features of the woman in Image 1 (Selfie).
Bio-Fidelity: Render high-fidelity skin physics: visible micro-pores, natural "satin-finish" skin hydration, and fine vellus hair catching the light ONLY along the jawline.
Expression (CRITICAL): Replicate the exact pouty, heavy-lidded, and blank yet confident expression from the provided reference. She looks into the mirror with a relaxed, slightly unimpressed or "bored" aesthetic, with full, relaxed lips and a neutral, direct gaze.
Silhouette Calibration: Amplify the dominant structural bust volume and the substantial, thick-toned leg presence. Focus on the aggressive hourglass structural tapering at the waist and the defined anatomical depth of the midriff.
Outfit & Physicality:

Clothing: A matching heather-grey jersey athletic set. The top is an ultra-crop long-sleeve, and the bottom is a pair of skin-tight micro-shorts.
Tension & Physics: The fabric shows extreme elastic tension and a vacuum-tight fit. Her RIGHT HAND is pulling down the waistband of the shorts slightly, creating realistic tension lines and a natural skin indentation on her hip.
Pose & Composition:

Pose: Standing frontally in a large circular mirror with a gold frame. Her torso is slightly arched to highlight the symmetrical curvature and athletic lines of her silhouette.
Action: Her LEFT HAND holds a white iPhone 17 Pro at eye level, capturing the full-body reflection.
Environment & Lighting:

Lighting: Realistic indoor overhead lighting that creates defining shadows under the bust and along the abdominal muscles, enhancing the 3D volume of her form.
Background (SHARP): The reflection reveals a detailed living area: a dark grey sectional sofa, a wall with a thermostat, and a messy pile of clothes on a chair.
Foreground: A dark wooden table in front of the mirror holds a mini gold-glitter decorative tree and scattered papers. Everything is perfectly IN FOCUS.
Quality: Raw unedited social-media dump aesthetic, high dynamic range, visible digital grain in the deep shadows, 8k resolution.
```

## Cinematic SUV Selfie Portrait with Lifestyle Inset

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A hyper-realistic, cinematic selfie portrait of a beautiful young woman seated in the driver’s seat of a luxury SUV. She has long, flowing wavy blonde hair with natural highlights, slightly tousled, framing her face organically. Her bright blue eyes are crystal clear with natural reflections, subtle eyeliner, soft mascara, and perfectly blended neutral-toned makeup. Her skin appears smooth and lifelike with realistic texture, soft pores, and gentle natural glow. She wears a fitted black short-sleeve top that contours naturally and a delicate silver necklace with a small minimalist pendant resting just above her collarbone. A seatbelt crosses diagonally across her chest, adding realism and casual authenticity.
The SUV interior features premium light gray leather upholstery, clean stitching, a modern dashboard softly out of focus, and a panoramic sunroof above. Sunlight streams through the windshield and sunroof, casting warm, soft shadows across her face and shoulders, creating a balanced natural lighting setup with cinematic depth. The camera angle is slightly above eye level, handheld smartphone selfie perspective, with shallow depth of field and razor-sharp facial focus.
In the top-left corner, a small rounded inset photo shows a serene suburban street lined with lush green trees, manicured lawns, and modern houses beneath a bright blue sky with scattered white clouds, adding a lifestyle storytelling element. Photorealistic rendering, ultra-detailed textures, HDR lighting, true-to-life color grading, professional DSLR quality, cinematic realism, 8K resolution, sharp focus, natural proportions.
```

## Realistic Low-Light Phone Selfie (Screen Glow)

> Minimal 3B sahne üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Vertical, realistic low-light phone selfie in a dim bedroom at night. Same face as reference (no changes). Cool bluish-purple screen glow lighting on a pale face; background mostly dark with beige blackout curtains on the left. Sleepy, cozy, late-night lo-fi vibe.
Close-up, slightly low angle. Subject lying on stomach, propped on a grey pillow with subtle pattern. Messy black bedhead hair with wispy bangs. Soft, dreamy/blank stare, mouth slightly open. Hand near face, index finger touching lowerlip.
Wearing a black sleeveless camisole; thin lace strap visible, left shoulder bare. Soft focus, visible grain/noise, high contrast between lit face and dark background.
Negative Prompt:
bright daylight, sunshine, studio/flash lighting, warm/orange tones, outdoor, sharp focus, professional camera, 4k clean, smooth skin, heavy makeup, cartoon, anime, 3D render, deformed hands, extra/missing fingers, out-of-focus face.
```

## Modern Indoor Mirror Selfie Portrait

> Minimal mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A natural, modern indoor mirror-selfie portrait of a young woman inside a stylish contemporary apartment. The image is captured at mid-to-full body length from a slightly angled side profile, reflected in a mirror. She holds a smartphone near her shoulder while taking the photo, gently turning her head back toward the mirror with a subtle, confident smile that feels relaxed and intimate.
Her expression is calm and self-assured, with relaxed eyes and softly lifted lips, conveyingeffortless confidence. She has a light-to-medium skin tone with natural texture, soft blush on the cheeks, and minimal makeup—defined brows, lightly enhanced eyes, and natural nude-toned lips. Her long dark brown hair is worn loose and straight with a slight natural wave, parted near the center, falling smoothly down her back with a few soft strands framing her face.
She wears a {argument name="clothing item" default="cropped light gray knit sweater"} with long sleeves, paired with high-waisted, loose-fitting blue denim jeans, creating a casual yet flattering silhouette. Her posture is relaxed and candid, body angled sideways with one shoulder slightly raised, reinforcing an authentic lifestyle feel.
The background features a clean, modern interior with warm wooden flooring, dark wood-paneled walls, a door and light switches, and subtle details such as a piano and a large window revealing a distant city skyline. The background remains softly out of focus to keep attention on the subject.
Lighting is warm, natural, and evenly diffused, illuminating her face and body without harsh shadows and creating a cozy, lived-in atmosphere. The color palette blends warm wood tones, soft grays, denim blues, and natural skin hues for a calm, contemporary, aesthetically balanced look.
The overall mood is intimate, modern, stylish, and effortlessly cool—like a quiet personal moment captured at home.
Use the uploaded image as the only and exact face reference. Preserve identical facial identity, structure, proportions, and expression with no alterations.
```

## Kindergarten Crayon Doodle Illustration Prompt

> Minimal, elle çizilmiş illüstrasyon üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Use the uploaded image strictly as the identity reference. Preserve the same person, facial structure, hairstyle, age, gender, body proportions, and likeness exactly. Do not add or remove any person. Do not change identity. Convert the subject into an ultra-cute kindergarten-style crayon doodle illustration.

Illustration Style:
Hand-drawn kindergarten doodle made with crayons and soft colored pencils. Lines should be thick, rounded, uneven, and slightly shaky, like a small child’s drawing. Outlines are imperfect, playful, and soft, never sharp or clean.

Color & Texture:
Bright pastel crayon colors with soft, chalky texture. Flat coloring with visible crayon grain and overlapping strokes. Colors may go slightly outside the lines to enhance the childlike feel. No realistic gradients; only simple color fills and soft watercolor-like blobs.

Facial Features:
Very simple and adorable facial features. Big round dot eyes, tiny curved smile, minimal or no nose. Cheeks can have soft pink crayon circles. Expression should feel innocent, happy, and friendly.

Proportions & Form:
Chibi-like doodle proportions — large head, small rounded body, short arms and legs. Hands and feet are simple mitten or oval shapes. Anatomy should look intentionally naive and cute.

Shading & Lighting:
No realistic lighting. No depth or shadows. If needed, add light pastel crayon scribbles under the character for grounding only.

Mood & Atmosphere:
Sweet, cozy, wholesome, and joyful. Feels like an illustration from a nursery book or a child’s school drawing. Very warm and comforting emotional tone.

Background:
Simple doodle background with crayon-drawn clouds, sun, stars, hearts, flowers, or soft scribbles. Use light pastel paper tones. Background must remain minimal and playful.

Rendering Rules:
No photorealism, no photo texture, no cinematic lighting, no digital painting, no anime, no manga, no 3D, no vector art. Everything must look hand-drawn with crayons and pencils.

Final Output:
An ultra-cute kindergarten-style crayon doodle illustration with childlike proportions, playful pastel colors, and innocent expressions, maintaining the exact identity from the uploaded image in a warm children’s storybook style.
```

She has a small silver nose septum piercing and extensive intricate blackwork tattoos covering her upper chest, shoulders, and arms (including detailed roses, skulls, and abstract patterns). Ultra-detailed skin texture with realistic subtle sheen, intricate tattoo details and ribbed fabric texture, sharp focus on face and upper body, shallow depth of field with gentle background blur, high dynamic range, 8K resolution, photorealism, masterpiece quality."

## Hyper-Detailed Structured Prompt for High-Fidelity Portrait Generation

```
      }

      }

      "expression": "Poised",

      "head_rotation": "Posterior_to_Anterior_Over_Shoulder"
    }
  },
  "kinematics_and_pose": {
    "skeletal_orientation": {
      "torso": "Facing_Away_180_degrees",
      "neck": "Rotated_toward_camera",
      "stance": "Static_Standing"
    },
    "spatial_anchor": {
      "coordinate_plane": "Outdoor_Porch_Balcony",
      "surface_interaction": "Weight_distributed_evenly"
    }
  },
  "material_science_wardrobe": {
    "primary_garment_top": {
      "classification": "Long_Sleeve_Crop_Top",
      "chroma": "{argument name=\"top color\" default=\"Tan_Light_Brown\"}",
      "textile_physics": {
        "weave": "Sheer_Knit",
        "transparency": "High_Subsurface_Scattering",
        "specular_map": "Sparkly_Crystalline_Glitter",

      }
    },
    "secondary_garment_bottom": {
      "classification": "High_Waisted_Denim_Shorts",
      "chroma": "Matte_Black",
      "textile_physics": {
        "weave": "Heavy_Denim_Twill",
        "edge_finish": "Raw_Cutoff_Frayed",
        "specular_map": "Zero_Reflectivity"
      }
    }
  },
  "world_engine_environment": {
    "architecture": {
      "type": "Rustic_Balcony_Porch",
      "materials": ["Wood_Grain", "Textured_Railing"]
    },
    "biome_specification": {
      "flora": ["Tall_Pine_Trees", "Evergreen_Conifers"],
      "density_index": "Maximum_Foliage_Saturation",
      "color_profile": "Deep_Forest_Greens"
    },
    "atmospheric_system": {
      "sky_state": "Grey_Overcast",
      "cloud_cover": "100%",
      "weather_simulation": "Muted_Diffusion_No_Sunlight"
    }
  },
  "optical_render_pipeline": {
    "photographic_sim": {
      "lens_profile": "85mm_Prime_Portrait",
      "aperture": "f/2.8_Shallow_Depth",
      "iso": "100_Clean",
      "shutter_speed": "1/250_Freeze_Motion"
    },
    "lighting_model": {
      "primary": "Global_Diffused_Overcast",
      "secondary": "Subtle_Micro_Highlights_on_Knit_Sparkles",
      "shadow_type": "Ambient_Occlusion_Soft"
    }
  },
  "constraint_engine": {
    "negative_prompt_array": [
      "Face_Transformation",
      "Body_Morphing",
      "Direct_Sunlight",
      "Blue_Sky"
    ]
  }
}
```

```
 "festive_accents": {

      "option_2": "Wearing subtle brown reindeer antler headband",
      "option_3": "Wearing a simple red knitted scarf loosely"
    },
    "visual_style": {
      "background": "Solid light grey or white studio background (No busy Christmas trees)",
      "lighting": "Soft studio photography light, clean shadows",
      "color_palette": "Grey, White, Skin tone, with a pop of Red"
    },
  },

    "illustration",

}
```

```
Nighttime photograph of a young woman taken with an early-2000s digital camera, Canon IXUS, strong flash, compact camera style\
(keep the face and features identical to the original 100%, do not modify).A young woman stands taking a photo of herself in a mirror inside a bedroom with a white–gray color tone, clean and minimal atmosphere. She stands turned slightly to the right, her left hand holding a digital camera up at face level while calmly looking at the camera screen. She has long, straight dark hair flowing down her back. She wears a white strapless top with a cute cartoon print on the front and puffed short pants with a gray checkered pattern, clearly revealing a slim figure with naturally large breasts.Behind her is a bed covered with light-colored bedding, with several brown and cream teddy bears neatly arranged. Beside the bed is a white shelf; on it is a framed photo of the same woman, with a cream-colored teddy bear placed in front. Next to it are books, a small box, and small minimalist accessories. The room lighting is soft with a cool tone, making the image look clean, calm, and orderly.Same hairstyle, with small strands of hair partially covering the face, remove the flower from the ear. Hair slightly blowing, chic, cool, and sexy, with lightly messy layers. Pink blush on the cheeks, with a soft rosy tone on the cheeks and nose. Lips in a pink-orange shade.Looking directly at the camera, cute expression, dreamy, innocent, and playful.Pose: running fingers through the hair in a sexy IXUS flash hitting the face directly.
```

## Add Santa Hat to Profile Picture (Image-to-Image)

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal profil görseli üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Using the user-uploaded reference image as the base, add a classic Christmas Santa hat to the subject.

Style match: preserve the original art style exactly (2D/3D/photorealistic/anime/cartoon), keeping identity, facial features, hairstyle, colors, shading, and overall composition unchanged.

Hat design: traditional Santa hat (red felt with subtle fabric texture, white fluffy trim, white pom-pom). Place it naturally on top of the head, aligned to head angle and hairline. Respect perspective, scale, and head shape.

Integration: match the existing lighting, shadows, highlights, linework, and rendering style. Add realistic contact shadows where the hat touches hair/forehead. Do not alter the face, hair, background, clothing, or any other elements.

Output: square (1:1) avatar, crisp and clear at small profile-picture size.
```

## Cinematic Mafia Boss Portrait Prompt

```
Use the uploaded face as the primary identity reference. 
Create a hyper-realistic, cinematic portrait of the subject as a modern mafia boss.

Scene & Composition:
– Subject seated inside a luxury black car (rear seat), window slightly open
– Wearing a tailored black suit, black shirt, no tie
– Tinted aviator sunglasses
– Smoking a thick cigar, dense smoke curling upward
– Cold, fearless, dominant expression
– Confident, intimidating posture

Lighting & Mood:
– Cool color tones ({argument name="color tones" default="blue/steel"})
– High contrast cinematic lighting
– Sharp rim light on face and cigar smoke
– Noir atmosphere

Background:
– Moody overcast sky
– Blurred city street / urban lights outside the car
– Strong bokeh and motion blur for depth
– Dark, cinematic ambience

Camera & Style:
Ultra-detailed facial realism
Sharp focus on face and smoke
Shallow depth of field
1:1 aspect ratio
Movie-poster composition
8K resolution look
Hyper-realistic, photoreal, cinematic grading
```

## Realistic 'Lazy-Hot' Mirror Selfie in Dorm Room

> Portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
(Vertical 9:16 smartphone aspect ratio). A highly realistic, casual full-body mirror selfie taken in a cozy, slightly messy bedroom or college dorm.

Subject: A cute 19-year-old Caucasian girl with a youthful, fresh face and long, messy-wavy blonde/light-brown hair.* 

Physique: She has a fit, curvy body with a slim waist and wider hips.*   

Pose: She stands in front of a tall floor mirror. She adopts a flattering pose: one leg is stepped slightly forward, and she pops her hip to the side, accentuating her curves. 

She holds an iPhone (with a cute case) up to the mirror. She is looking at the phone screen in the reflection, not the camera.

Outfit (The "Lazy-Hot" Look):*   

Top: A tight, white ribbed tank top or baby tee, cropped short to show her midriff and belly button.*   

Bottom: Grey sweatpants worn low-rise (rolled down at the waist) to highlight her waist-to-hip ratio.

Environment: The room is a typical teenager's bedroom. Unmade bed in the background, soft LED strip lights (purple or warm white) on the ceiling, and maybe some clothes on a chair. It feels lived-in and authentic.

Lighting: Natural light coming from a window mixed with the soft indoor room light, creating a flattering, soft glow on her skin.
```

## Hyper-Realistic POV Selfie with Bedhead Aesthetic

> Fotogerçekçi portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A hyper-realistic, extreme close-up POV selfie of the same woman from the reference photo, lying down and pressing her left cheek into a fluffy cream-colored faux-fur pillow that gently squishes against her face. Her head is tilted to the right as she gazes softly toward the camera with a sleepy, sultry, intimate expression — half-closed, heavy-lidded eyes and slightly parted lips.Her hair appears naturally messy in a relaxed “bedhead” state, with wispy strands falling across her forehead and partially over her left eye, without altering its original appearance from the reference. She has dewy, glowing skin with visible natural texture, a cold-girl makeup aesthetic featuring soft rosy blush on the nose tip and cheeks, subtly blurred pink-toned lips, and neatly groomed brows. She is wearing a single large white pearl stud earring on her exposed ear and a very thin, delicate gold chain necklace, with one bare shoulder visible. The scene is lit by soft, warm directional window light coming from the right, creating a golden-hour glow with gentle shadows on the left side of her face and highlights along the bridge of her nose, cheekbone, and lower lip. Shot on an iPhone front-facing camera at 23mm, f/2.2, from a high-angle selfie perspective with shallow depth of field and soft bokeh. Finished with a VSCO C1 or G3-style filter — warm tones, slightly desaturated blacks, subtle film grain, low contrast — creating a dreamy, hazy, lazy Sunday morning atmosphere.
```

## Pair Fashion Generation Prompt

> Minimal moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
[Setting Section]
[Female Outfit Name]: {argument name="female outfit name" default="Sailor Uniform"}
[Hair Color]: {argument name="hair color" default="Black Hair"}
[Eye Color]: {argument name="eye color" default="Blue Eyes"}
[Theme Color]: {argument name="theme color" default="Light Blue and White"}
[Style Atmosphere]: Delicate anime coloring, cleanliness, character standing illustration on a white background

[Generation Prompt Body]
Highest quality animation illustration.
One boy (male character). Full body view.
Hair color is [Hair Color], eye color is [Eye Color].

《Outfit Instructions》
He is wearing a male outfit that is socially considered a "pair" or counterpart to the [Female Outfit Name].
・Important: If the [Female Outfit Name] is a design specific to women (e.g., Sailor Uniform, Dress, Furisode), do not apply the female design directly; convert it to the corresponding male formal wear or uniform.
(Conversion Examples: Sailor Uniform to "Gakuran" or "Blazer", Furisode to "Montsuki Hakama", Dress to "Tuxedo")
・The outfit's color scheme should incorporate the [Theme Color] to create a sense of pairing.

The art style is modern anime style with delicate lines and vibrant colors, similar to the attached image, and the background is simple white.
```

Ultra-detailed skin texture with subtle sweat sheen, realistic mesh fabric details and hand wrap texture, sharp focus on face and upper body, slight depth of field with gentle background blur, high dynamic range, 8K resolution, photorealism, masterpiece quality.

```
Photorealistic close-up selfie of a beautiful young woman with long braids blonde hair parted in the middle, white cat ears, fair skin with a subtle natural glow, striking green eyes with bold winged eyeliner, long lashes, and glossy deep nude-pink lips in a confident smile. She has a small silver nose septum piercing, multiple delicate layered silver necklaces including a cursive '{argument name="pendant name" default="Keor"}' pendant and small star charms. Her upper chest, shoulders, and arms are covered in extensive intricate blackwork tattoos featuring roses, skulls, floral designs, and abstract patterns. She wears a fitted white sports bra with thin straps and a deep scoop neckline that reveals her toned midriff and cleavage, paired with high-waisted lavender pastel athletic leggings that hug her curves. Pose: standing in a cozy bedroom taking a low-angle selfie with one arm extended holding the phone, body angled slightly to show her figure, one hand resting on her hip, looking directly at the camera with a playful, seductive gaze. Background: soft-lit bedroom with light gray walls, a gray upholstered headboard, beige chunky knit throw, pink salt lamp, and decorative pillows, warm natural daylight creating gentle highlights on her skin and tattoos. Ultra-detailed skin texture with realistic freckles and glow, intricate tattoo details, sharp focus on face and upper body, shallow depth of field with gentle background blur, high dynamic range, 8K resolution, photorealism, masterpiece quality.
```

```
"shadows":"{argument name="shadows" default="+12"}",

"vignette":"Soft",
"color_grade":"Warm beige vintage tint",
"sharpness":"Slightly reduced to mimic old camera softness"
},

"global_context":{
"scene_description":"Indoor hallway with wall decor, mirror reflection, white walls and warm flash lighting.",
"time_of_day":"Evening",
"lighting":"Direct warm flash illuminating the subject with strong falloff into the background.",
"environment_details":[
"Mirror behind the subject with reflection visible.",
"White paneled walls.",
"Marble/stone console table with a floral arrangement.",
"Soft shadows created by harsh flash."
]
},

"composition":{
"camera_angle":"Eye-level, straight-on portrait",
"framing":"Knees-up full portrait",

"expression":"Confident, pouty, soft glamorous gaze.",
"flash_effect":"Glossy highlights on skin and dress."
},

"subject":{

"style":"Soft glam.",
"lips":"{argument name="lip color" default="Pink-brown gloss with warm undertone"}.",
"eyes":"Eyeliner + lashes.",
"blush":"Warm peach."
},
"skin":"Natural undertone, realistic texture, NOT smoothed.",
"outfit":{
"dress":"{argument name="dress description" default="Black mini halter dress with deep neckline and satin-like shine"}.",
"fabric_behavior":"Flash creates bright reflections and visible folds."
}
},

"background_objects":[
{
"id":"mirror",
"description":"Large wall mirror reflecting the subject's pose.",
"lighting":"Flash reflection visible but subdued."
},
{
"id":"flowers",
"description":"Vibrant bouquet on the table ({argument name="flower colors" default="yellow, purple, green"})."
},
{
"id":"wall",
"description":"White paneled wall with framed signage."
}
],

"rules":{
"identity_constant": true,
"no_face_change": true,
"no_ai_artifacts": true,
"realism":"Ultra realistic evening flash aesthetic with vintage tones."
}
}
```

```
{
"meta":{
"quality":"High-Definition",

},

"lighting":"{argument name="lighting" default="Direct warm flash illuminating the subject with strong falloff into the background."}",

"{argument name="environment detail 2" default="White paneled walls."}",

"expression":"{argument name="expression" default="Confident, pouty, soft glamorous gaze."}",
"flash_effect":"{argument name="flash effect" default="Glossy highlights on skin and dress."}"
},

"subject":{
"makeup":{

},

"background_objects":[
{
"id":"mirror",
```

## Ultra-Photorealistic Saree Mirror Selfie

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Ultra photorealistic 8K candid smartphone mirror selfie of a young adult woman with warm natural skin tone, visible realistic skin texture, subtle highlights, and no plastic or over-smoothed look. She has dark brown hair, loose with natural volume, smooth texture, and a middle part. Her expression is a soft confident gaze directly at the camera, natural lips slightly parted. Pose is relaxed and natural, slightly angled body with shoulders down, like a casual mirror selfie taken indoors. She is wearing an elegant black saree with soft realistic fabric folds and draping physics, paired with a sleeveless black blouse that has subtle shimmer embroidery, accessorized with minimal jewelry and a delicate waist chain. Setting is a modern indoor bedroom or dressing area with clean white wardrobe cabinets in the background, softly blurred. Lighting is soft overhead indoor ceiling light creating balanced natural shadows and highlights. Camera angle slightly above eye level, natural portrait lens with sharp focus on the subject and realistic depth of field. The entire image must look like a genuine real-life photo—editorial yet completely natural and candid, no cartoon style, no AI artifacts, no distortions especially on hands or limbs, no extra limbs, no text or watermarks, highly detailed realism.
(Aspect ratio 9:16 vertical)
```

## Identity-Locked Mirror Selfie Pose Recreation Prompt

> Fotogerçekçi portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
{
  "template_id": "mirror_side_pose_workout_shorts_ultralock_v1_compact",
  "version": "1.0.1",
  "intent": "Recreate a mirror selfie of a woman in a fitted crop t-shirt and high-waisted workout shorts, allowing only identity replacement while locking all scene parameters.",
  "subject_identity_lock": {
    "replace_identity_only": true,
    "preserve_body_proportions": true,
    "preserve_skin_tone_range": true,
    "preserve_hair_length_category": "medium-long",
    "hair_color_band": "light-brown_with_highlights",
    "face_direction_degrees": {
      "yaw_right": 55,
      "pitch_down": 8,
      "roll": 0
    },
    "expression_tone": "soft_neutral",
    "age_band_years": [
      20,
      35
    ]
  },
  "camera_lock": {
    "lens_focal_length_mm": 26,
    "sensor_format": "full_frame_eq",
    "camera_distance_m": 1.2,
    "camera_height_m": 1.45,
    "camera_tilt_degrees": {
      "pitch_down": -2
    },
    "framing": {
      "aspect_ratio": "9:16",
      "subject_fill_fraction": 0.8,
      "center_bias_xy": [
        0.52,
        0.52
      ]
    },
    "mirror_geometry": {
      "plane_alignment": "parallel",
      "distance_m": 0.6,
      "dimensions_m": [
        0.9,
        2.1
      ]
    }
  },
  "phone_lock": {
    "hand": "right",
    "position_xy": [
      0.38,
      0.47
    ],
    "orientation_degrees": {
      "yaw": 2
    },
    "grip": "fingers_wrapped_thumb_along_edge"
  },
  "pose_lock": {
    "global_orientation": {
      "pelvis_yaw_deg": 80,
      "torso_yaw_deg": 65,
      "head_yaw_deg": 55
    },
    "spine": {
      "lumbar_curve": "soft",
      "thoracic_curve": "upright"
    },
    "legs": {
      "supporting": {
        "side": "left",
        "knee_deg": 8,
        "foot_angle_deg": 12,
        "foot_contact": "flat"
      },
      "rear": {
        "side": "right",
        "knee_deg": 22,
        "toe_contact": "forefoot",
        "heel_elevation_cm": 2.5
      }
    },
    "arms": {
      "right": {
        "shoulder_flex_deg": 35,
        "elbow_flex_deg": 90,
        "hand_pos_xy": [
          0.38,
          0.47
        ]
      },
      "left": {
        "visibility": "minimal"
      }
    },
    "hips": {
      "pelvis_tilt_deg": 6,
      "weight_distribution": [
        0.8,
        0.2
      ]
    },
    "neck_head": {
      "chin_down_deg": 6,
      "gaze": "toward_phone"
    }
  },
  "clothing_geometry_lock": {
    "top": {
      "type": "crop_tshirt",
      "color": "soft_white",
      "sleeves": "short",
      "hem": "above_navel",
      "fabric": {
        "stretch": "horizontal",
        "wrinkle_zones": [
          "under_bust",
          "side_seams"
        ]
      }
    },
    "bottom": {
      "type": "workout_shorts_high_waist",
      "coverage": "full",
      "inseam_cm": 7,
      "color": "lilac_with_subtle_stripes",
      "waistband_cm": 6,
      "leg_opening_ratio": 0.85,
      "stretch_zones": [
        "glutes",
        "outer_hips"
      ]
    }
  }
}
```

```
{
  "meta": {
    "project": "Egirl_FrogPose_PinkHK_Flux_V4.2",
    "target_engine": "Flux.1 [dev] / Nano Banana Pro",
    "version": "4.2.0 (Everything in Focus - f/11)",

  },

    "model": {
      "base": "flux1-dev.safetensors",
      "quantization": "fp8 / nf4",
      "vae": "ae.safetensors"
    },

      {
        "name": "Realism_LoRA_v2 (Optional)",
        "strength": 0.5,
        "note": "Enhances porcelain skin tone and fabric textures."
      }
    ],
    "sampling": {
      "sampler_name": "euler",
      "scheduler": "simple",
      "steps": 28,
      "guidance_scale": 2.5,
      "shift": 1.0
    },

      "width": 1024,
      "height": 1536,
      "aspect_ratio": "2:3",
      "megapixel_class": "1.5MP"
    }
  },
  "prompt_construction": {
    "narrative_layer": {
      "style": "E-Girl Mirror Selfie / Minimalist Goth",
      "instruction": "Capture a sharp, full-body mirror selfie of a young woman sitting in a frog pose on a bed, focusing on the midriff and the pink phone case.",
      "subject_flow": "A pale 19-21 year old woman with black wolf-cut hair sitting on a grey bed with knees up, holding a pink Hello Kitty phone."
    },
    "texture_layer": {
      "skin_physics": "pale porcelain skin, smooth texture, rosy blush, glossy lips, e-girl eyeliner",
      "fabric_physics": "ribbed knit texture of black tube top, metal grommets on belt, cotton texture of white socks, quilted grey duvet",
      "environment_physics": "SHARP DETAILS ON BACKGROUND: glass mirror reflection, white wall texture, window glass, grey fabric folds"
    },

      "focus": "DEEP DEPTH OF FIELD (f/11) - NO BLUR. The woman, the bed texture, and the background wall are all sharp.",
      "settings": "Smartphone Simulation, 24mm Lens, 1/60s, ISO 300 (Diffused Window Light)"
    },
    "color_grading": {
      "white_balance": "Cool Natural (Diffused Window Light)",
      "shadows": "Soft shadows defining the midriff",
      "highlights": "Soft highlights on skin and glossy phone case"
    }
  },
  "final_prompt_string": "A candid raw mirror selfie photograph shot on Smartphone f/11. Deep depth of field, everything in focus. A very slim 19-21 year old woman with pale porcelain skin and black hair with bangs (wolf cut) sitting on a dark grey bed in a 'frog pose' (knees bent, high and wide apart). She leans back slightly. She wears a black ribbed strapless tube top exposing her toned midriff, a low-rise black mini skirt with a heavy silver grommet belt, and white ankle socks with red Hello Kitty embroidery. She holds a phone with a light pink 3D Hello Kitty case. Dramatic e-girl eyeliner and glossy lips. Background is sharp and detailed: messy grey duvet and a plain white wall are clearly visible with no bokeh. "
```

## Cozy Yule Tide Selfie Portrait

> Mimari görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
A high-angle selfie perspective captures a young woman with a light skin tone and a subtle tan, sitting on a dark hardwood floor partially covered by a plush white faux fur rug, with one arm extended toward the lens holding the camera. Her legs are bent and tucked to the side. She wears a chunky cream-colored cable-knit sweater dress with a thick, textured knit pattern and an off-the-shoulder neckline that exposes her shoulders and collarbone, finished with long, oversized sleeves. Her long, voluminous blonde hair falls in loose waves with a side part. Her makeup features defined eyebrows, striking winged eyeliner, glossy nude lips, and soft contouring. She holds a soft, alluring gaze, her head tilted slightly to the right.

The setting is a richly cozy, dimly lit living room interior designed for warmth and comfort. Behind her is a substantial, rough-cut natural stone fireplace constructed with professional masonry, its uneven stone surface catching the firelight as bright orange flames burn steadily within. The surrounding space features dark, polished hardwood floors and well-appointed, comfortable furniture with a lived-in, upscale lodge-like feel. In the background, a decorated Christmas tree glows softly, strung with warm white lights that fall into gentle bokeh blur in the distance, adding depth and seasonal atmosphere without distracting from the subject. Christmas presents with colorful wrapping surround the base of the Christmas tree. The overall interior feels intimate, inviting, and quietly luxurious.

Lighting is dominated by a direct on-camera flash, producing crisp highlights on the subject and pronounced shadows cast behind her, while the fireplace provides a secondary warm ambient glow that subtly fills the room. The image aesthetic evokes early-2000s digital camera photography blended with influencer lifestyle imagery, capturing a cozy winter evening mood through a palette of warm neutrals, creamy whites, amber firelight, and deep brown shadows.
```

## High-Contrast Monochrome Studio Portrait of a Man

> Sinematik, minimal portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create a monochrome studio portrait of a man. Use minimal lighting with soft falloff so the focus stays entirely on him, with subtle gradients and deep shadows shaping his face. The composition should be a tight-medium shot against a clean, distraction-free background. He’s dressed in a casual, stylish outfit—like a simple tee or sweatshirt with light texture that works well in black and white. The overall mood should feel calm and confident, with a pleasing, balanced vibe. Render it in high-contrast black and white with crisp details, smooth tones, and a subtle cinematic depth.
```

## 90s Selfie Emotion Grid with Identity Lock

> Fotogerçekçi, retro portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
{
  "request_metadata": {
    "model": "Nano Banana Pro",
    "content_type": "photorealistic_90s_selfie_emotion_grid",
    "quality_preset": "ultra",
    "format_aspect_ratio": "1:1",
    "output": "3x3_grid"
  },

  "layout": {
    "type": "3x3_grid",
    "description": "Same person in all 9 frames. Keep identity locked. Same room, same curtain backdrop, same outfit, same lighting. Only facial expression/emotion + micro-gesture changes per frame.",
    "frame_labels": [
      "1) Flirty bite + wink",
      "2) Happy smile",
      "3) Angry",
      "4) Crying",
      "5) Blowing a kiss",
      "6) Playful wink",
      "7) Surprised",
      "8) Serious/neutral",
      "9) Smirk/confident"
    ]
  },

  "source": {
    "mode": "image_reference",
    "reference_images": {
      "primary": "base_person_image"
    },
    "preserve": {
      "same_person": true,
      "identity_lock": true,
      "keep_face": true,
      "keep_hair": true,
      "keep_outfit": true,
      "keep_skin_tone": true,
      "keep_freckles": true,
      "keep_eye_color": true,
      "keep_earrings": true
    }
  },

  "global_scene": {
    "shot_type": "close-up selfie portrait",
    "camera_pose": "right hand holding the camera for selfie, arm slightly forward",
    "body_pose": "body tilted slightly to the right",
    "environment": "a room with a grayish-white curtain backdrop, soft out-of-focus folds",
    "lighting": {
      "type": "natural_window_light",
      "direction": "front",
      "quality": "soft but bright",
      "notes": "keep consistent across all 9 frames; no lighting changes between frames"
    },
    "style": {
      "look": "90s camera selfie",
      "grain": "subtle film grain",
      "flash_feel": "mild on-camera flash feel (soft pop, not harsh)",
      "realism": "authentic candid realism"
    },
    "quality": {
      "detail": "high detail",
      "skin_texture": "natural skin texture, visible pores, realistic freckles",
      "retouching": "minimal; avoid smoothing/beauty filter"
    },
    "composition_lock": {
      "keep_framing": true,
      "keep_camera_distance": true,
      "keep_head_position": "only micro-variations allowed",
      "keep_background": true
    }
  },

  "subject": {
    "gender": "female",
    "age_group": "young_adult",
    "identity": "EXACT same face as reference (do not change facial structure, eyes, nose, lips, jawline)",
    "hair": "dark brown with highlights, high messy bun, bangs circling the forehead",
    "accessories": "diamond hoop earrings",
    "skin_details": "freckles around nose and cheekbones",
    "makeup": {
      "style": "natural",
      "blush": "subtle pink",
      "eyeliner": "defined cat-eye",
      "lashes": "curled",
      "lip": "glossy nude",
      "eyes": "brownish-green"
    },
    "outfit": "strapless black knit mini dress"
  },

  "frames": [
    {
      "id": 1,
      "emotion": "flirtatious",
      "expression_action": "biting her index finger g"
    }
  ]
}
```

## Early 2000s Digital Camera Flash Portrait Prompt

> Retro portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Use the facial features of the attached photo. A close-up shot of a young man displayed on the screen of a compact {argument name="camera brand" default="Canon"} digital camera. The camera body surrounds the image with its buttons, dials, and textured surface visible, including the FUNC/SET wheel, DISP button, and the “IMAGE STABILIZER” label along the side.

The photo on the screen shows the young man indoors at night, illuminated by a bright built-in flash that creates sharp highlights on his face and hair. He has short, slightly messy dark hair with loose strands framing part of his forehead, and a soft, slightly open-lip expression.

The flash accentuates his features against a dim, cluttered kitchen background with appliances, shelves, and metallic surfaces softly blurred. The mood is candid, raw, nostalgic, and reminiscent of early 2000s digital camera snapshots. Colors are slightly muted with cool undertones, strong flash contrast, and natural grain from the display. No text, no logos inside the photo preview itself.

Scale ratio: 4:5 vertical
Camera: compact digital camera simulation
Lens: equivalent to 28–35mm
Aperture: f/2.8
ISO: 400
Shutter speed: 1/60 with flash
White balance: auto flash
Lighting: harsh direct flash on subject, ambient low light in the background
Color grading: nostalgic digital camera tones, high contrast flash, subtle display grain, authentic screen glow.
```

## Cyberpunk Rooftop Selfie with Cold Stare

> Fütüristik portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Use the uploaded image as the reference for the subject's face and likeness. Do not alter facial features; maintain 100% feature consistency. Generate the image in a Vertical 9:16 aspect ratio. The image must be strictly taller than it is wide (portrait orientation) to simulate a full-screen smartphone photo.
The subject is taking a POV selfie with one arm visibly stretched out towards the camera lens. The camera device itself is not visible. She is looking directly into the lens with a 'Cold Stare' expression—sharp direct eye contact, lips closed, icy and powerful. The camera angle is a dynamic Dutch Angle (tilted).
The setting is a 'Rooftop Night' with a glass railing behind her and a bokeh city skyline in the distance. The atmosphere is a 'Neon Night,' with {argument name="neon colors" default="blue and pink"} light casting a cyberpunk glow on her skin. In the background, blurred by deep bokeh, there is a hint of 'Nightlife' activity with people standing in line.
She is wearing a rich {argument name="top color" default="burgundy"} 'Velvet Off-Shoulder Bustier' paired with a high-waisted black 'Leather Mini' skirt. Her hair is styled in a severe 'Slicked-Back Bun.' Her makeup focuses on 'Siren Eyes' with elongated eyeliner.
Her free hand is holding a 'Takeaway Cocktail' in a clear plastic cup with a neon straw and glowing ice cubes. On the wrist of the stretched arm, she wears a 'Thick Silver Curb Chain' bracelet. Her nails are long 'Vampy Stiletto' style in deep black. She wears a 'Statement Silver' cuban link chain. The image has a sharp modern smartphone aesthetic.
```

## Aerial Beach Selfie with Cheeky Wink

> Portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Subject:
A young woman with a sun-kissed, fair complexion and a constellation of freckles most prominent across the bridge of her nose and cheeks, rendered with Deep Fusion texture accuracy. Her hair is a cascade of long, tousled brown waves. She has one eye open—a sharp, captivating green/hazel orb with intricate iris detail—and one eye closed in a wink. Her eyebrows are full, dark, and naturally shaped.

Attire & Details:

She wears a playful black bikini top with classic white polka dots. A delicate, layered gold chain necklace rests at her neck, catching the light.

Expression & Pose:

Her expression is irresistibly cheeky and playful. She winks with her right eye (viewer's left), her tongue peeking out slightly at the corner of her smiling mouth, revealing a glimpse of teeth. Her right hand rests casually atop her head, fingers lightly buried in her hair. The frame is a close-up, selfie-style portrait where the curve of her arm holding the device is visible, creating an intimate, immediate feel.

Environment & New Shot Setup:

She is in the sea, swimming. The shot is now captured from above by a drone. Below her stretches clear, turquoise water. The background is a tropical beach or coastal setting with blurred vibrant green palm leaves or a sandy shoreline.

Lighting:

The scene is bathed in bright, natural daylight. Sunlight filtering through leaves above creates soft, dynamic patterns of light and shadow on the water's surface and on the young woman, expertly balanced by Smart HDR processing to preserve detail in both highlights and shadows. Sparkling glints and reflections on the water add visual richness.

Technical Specifications:

Device: iPhone 17 Pro Max (simulating Drone Control/Recording)

Camera: Main or Telephoto Lens (suited for top-down shot)

Lens: 48mm or 77mm equivalent, wide aperture

Shot Details: 48MP ProRAW / HEIF Max

Processing: Photonic Engine, Deep Fusion, Smart HDR 6

Style: Standard Photographic Style, leveraging extreme dynamic range

Quality Tags: Ultra-fine facial detail, natural skin texture, tack-sharp focus, 8K resolution, mobile photography masterpiece, 'Shot on iPhone' aesthetic, aerial shot, over-water composition.
```

## Early 2000s Digital Camera Mirror Selfie

> Fotogerçekçi, minimal portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create a hyper-realistic photograph of a woman (100% faithful to the original face reference, no alterations or beautification allowed) taking a mirror selfie in her bedroom using a smartphone camera. 

She has her original hairstyle with some loose, slightly messy strands of hair softly falling over her face in a cool, effortlessly chic yet sexy way, with good volume. She’s wearing a {argument name="top style" default="vintage-style spaghetti-strap crop top"} and {argument name="bottom style" default="high-waisted denim shorts"}. She’s cradling a {argument name="pet type" default="fluffy orange tabby cat"} in her arms.

Image style: Shot on an {argument name="camera type" default="early-2000s digital camera"}, using only natural light, no flash. Soft late-afternoon golden sunlight streaming through the window, gently hitting her skin, face, and the cat’s fur, creating warm natural highlights and glows. Slightly warm yellow-orange-gray tone, subtle digital noise and grain for that authentic vintage digital look.

Composition: Half-body mirror shot, focus sharply on her face and the cat as the main subjects. Background shows a simple white bed and plain walls, with soft patches of sunlight falling across the wall and bed, contrasted by gentle shadows from the angled light. Overall framing feels like a casual, candid early-2000s snapshot.

Overall atmosphere: Warm, natural, unposed, and intimate. The natural light enhances the retro digital vintage vibe — charming, approachable, and nostalgic, like a simple everyday moment captured on a sunny afternoon in the 2000s.
```

## Image Annotation and Praise Commentary Prompt

> Elle çizilmiş moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Add messy red handwritten Japanese comments, expressive doodles, and energetic editorial-style notes directly on top of the image, as if written with a red marker over printed photos. The handwriting should feel spontaneous, unfiltered, emotional, slightly chaotic, and personal—almost like someone’s private photo journal or annotated scrapbook page.

All handwritten text must:
– be in Japanese
– appear hand-drawn, uneven, casual, and overlapping
– be positive in tone

The content of each handwritten note must automatically analyze the uploaded image and praise the woman based on what is visible, such as her outfit, expression, pose, styling, makeup, or mood.
The comments should feel affectionate, admiring, or supportive, and tailored to what stands out in the photo.

Do not repeat generic praise.
Instead, generate unique personal compliments that match the image attributes.

Examples must NOT be shown explicitly in the prompt.
Instead, describe the logic:

“The system should observe the clothing style, face, mood, posture, accessories, or color choices and produce handwritten comments that positively highlight what is visually appealing or admirable about her.”

Also add:
– arrows pointing to notable fashion details
– underlines beneath interesting areas
– circled elements
– crossed-out planning notes like editing marks
– layered cut-and-paste collage scraps (magazine style)
– small torn paper labels
– sticker-like notations
– tape-texture fragments

Overall direction:
Create a visually intense scrapbook-journal composition, filled with affectionate handwriting, red editorial markings, and collage fragments, giving the entire image an emotional and expressive “personal praise commentary” aesthetic.
```

## Y2K Flash Portrait in Christmas Café Prompt

> Fotogerçekçi, retro moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create a hyper-realistic portrait of a cute woman using my reference image. She is sitting with her chin resting on her hand at a dark wooden table in front of a vintage-style café.

Fashion & Outfit:
She wears a shiny black leather jacket, slightly open to reveal a white inner top, showing a beautiful neckline. Matched with a fitted black mini-skirt set. She also wears black fishnet stockings. Add a fluffy fur hat over her outfit.

Background:
In front of a marble-grey café exterior, decorated with a Christmas theme. Include a realistic Christmas tree, pine decorations, and festive ornaments.

Lighting & Tone:
Warm orange tungsten lighting for an inviting, cozy atmosphere, combined with a strong direct flash hitting the subject straight on, making her skin bright and glowing, contrasting with the slightly darker background.
The photo should feel Y2K digital compact-camera style—sharp 8K resolution, realistic skin texture, with light film grain.

Hair & Makeup:
Long dark-brown hair blowing slightly in the wind, messy layered strands with a few pieces softly covering part of her face—stylish, cool, and subtly sexy.
Her skin is very fair.
Makeup style: Douyin/Korean—long curled lashes, soft flushed pink blush on cheeks and nose tip, glossy pink lips.
She has Christmas-themed nail art.

Props:
On the table:
• A large bouquet wrapped in brown kraft paper, containing cotton flowers and pine leaves.
• A gold Canon IXY 30S camera placed on the table.

She holds a Christmas-decorated drink in her right hand and smiles slightly.

Additional Visual Style:
The flash creates extremely bright, slightly yellow-tinted skin with a hard shadow behind her.
High-contrast, ultra-sharp 8K image.
Warm ambient sunlight.
Fuji Film Pro 400H color style with old-lens filter and light film grain.
The subject should appear bright and stand out clearly from the background.
```

## Head-top Diorama Prompt for Nano Banana Pro Agent V1.2 (Afro/Cloud Amusement Park)

> Manzara üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
You are a **"Miniature Set Designer and Portrait Photographer" who constructs fantastical worlds.** Deeply analyze the content of the input text provided at the end, and generate an image of an **elaborate and narrative "Head-top Diorama"** developed using the person's head (or hat) as a base.

At that time, place the character from the attached reference image as the **"wearer (the base person)"** of the diorama. Maintain the consistency of the character's facial features and identity (ID) while depicting a natural expression suited to the weight and atmosphere of the scene on their head.

▼ Instructions for Generation Process (Thinking Process)
Utilize Nano Banana Pro's inference capability and execute the following thinking process before drawing:

1. **[Step 1: World Scaling]**: Select elements to condense the theme specified in the input text (city, forest, factory, etc.) to hat size.
2. **[Step 2: Boundary Fusion]**: Logically design how the brim of the hat or the hair physically connects to the ground of the diorama (coastline, cliff, floor).
3. **[Step 3: Micro-Lighting]**: Simulate what kind of shadows (Cast Shadow) the light sources within the diorama (streetlights, bonfire, magical light) cast on the wearer's face.

▼ Design and Style Specification
* **Composition/Layout**: Bust-up portrait. The camera is slightly high-angle (俯瞰) to show details on the head. Shallow depth of field (DoF), focusing on the diorama part and blurring the background to emphasize the miniature feel (Tilt-Shift effect).
* **Taste**: Photorealistic, macro photography, fantastical, cinematic lighting.
* **Text**: Accurately draw text symbolizing the theme (e.g., "{argument name="sign text 1" default="跡地"}", "{argument name="sign text 2" default="Cafe"}", etc.) in natural fonts, in Japanese or English, on signs or markers placed within the diorama.
* **Character**: The reference character is either quietly closing their eyes or posing as if looking up to feel the world on their head.

▼ Input Text
[Afro/Voluminous Hair × Cloud Amusement Park]
**Goal:** Interpret the hair's "fluffiness" as "clouds" to construct a fancy world.
> **Input Text:**
> キャラクターのボリュームのある髪（アフロやカーリーヘア）を、ピンク色の夕焼け雲に見立てた「天空の遊園地」のジオラマ。
> * **風景:** 髪の雲の上に、観覧車やメリーゴーランド、ジェットコースターのレールが埋まっている。風船がいくつも空へ飛んでいく。
> * **光の演出:** 夕日が逆光となり、髪（雲）の輪郭が金色に輝く（リムライト）。遊園地のイルミネーションがキラキラと点灯し始めている。
> * **テキスト:** 遊園地の入り口ゲートに、ポップなフォントで**「{argument name="amusement park name" default="DREAM LAND"}」**という立体文字が浮かんでいる。
> * **詳細:** 髪の柔らかい質感が、綿菓子や雲のようなエーテル状の物質に見える.
```

## Four Sketch and Portrait Prompts for Nano Banana Pro

> Fotogerçekçi, elle çizilmiş portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Prompt 1:
"Ultra-realistic 8K mid-shot portrait. Use the exact face from the uploaded photo.
Warm, romantic "golden-hour muse" aesthetic - Vogue Fine Arts x Italian painting studio.
Hair: soft loose waves, natural volume, middle/side part, warm golden glow on strands.
Makeup: dewy luminous skin, golden highlighter, peach eyeshadow, long fluffy lashes, rosy watercolor blush, glossy peach-rose lips.
Outfit: cream/soft beige off-shoulder knit or white draped top (not black, not oversized). Exposed collarbone. Thin gold necklace, tiny pearl earrings, subtle gold rings.
Background: one HUGE colorful oil painting of her face, filling the entire background.
Soft warm brushstrokes, golden/peach/blush tones, creamy texture, flowing hair strokes, Renaissance-meets-modern glow. Rich, emotional, romantic.
Pose: mid-shot, slightly off-center. Exposed shoulder toward camera. Hands gently touching arm/table. Head slightly down, eyes softly lifted. Dreamy, warm, romantic expression.
Studio: warm artist's workspace - wooden desk, jars with brushes, oil paints, smeared warm pigments, golden sunlight from the side, dust in the light, reflections on wet oil paint.
Lighting: golden-hour soft warmth, gentle shadows, glowing skin + glowing painting.
Camera: Canon EOS R5, 50mm f/1.2, f/1.2, ISO 160. Luminous skin, creamy tones, painterly softness.
Exclude: no charcoal, no dark mood, no black clothing, entirely new warm aesthetic."

Prompt 2:
"Convert the Uploaded image into a Highly detailed sketch-style illustration  in a powerful yet peaceful pose.
Uploaded image with a beautiful dress well-defined same pose a exuding devotion and inner strength. The same dress with soft shading and artistic texture. She is holding her phone up with one hand to capture the photo. Her outfit is a tight {argument name="outfit type" default="black short-sleeve bodycon dress"} that fits snugly around her waist and hips. She is accessorized with simple {argument name="jewelry color" default="gold"} jewelry, including bracelets and a ring. The setting is a modern, minimal bathroom with light neutral walls, a glass shower, folded towels, a marble countertop, and soft natural lighting. The photo should look exactly like a realistic phone selfie with accurate reflections, sharp detail, and natural skin texture. ## Neon-Lit Mirror Reflection Portrait Prompt
```

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
[1] SUBJECT:
beautiful young woman, standing sideways, facing right, soft expression,
wearing a tight teal off-shoulder ribbed dress, slim body shape,
loose blonde hair tied casually, soft strands falling near face

[2] SCENE & COMPOSITION:
full-body/three-quarter portrait,
standing next to a tall reflective glass wall,
perfect mirror reflection visible,
symmetrical reflection effect,
camera positioned at slight lower angle to create depth

[3] LIGHTING:
warm orange neon lights in the background,
cool teal light reflecting from below,
cinematic lighting blend (orange + teal),
soft rim light on shoulders and hair,
subtle glow, atmospheric depth

[4] BACKGROUND:
urban night scene, blurred parking lot lights,
horizontal glowing bokeh lines,
creamy bokeh, shallow depth of field,
smooth gradient colors (orange, yellow, teal)

[5] CAMERA & LENS:
shot on 85mm f/1.4,
shallow DOF,
DSLR full-frame look,
soft cinematic contrast, film-like texture,
high dynamic range, 8K clarity

[6] STYLE & QUALITY:
hyper-realistic skin texture,
smooth highlights,
professional portrait photography,
cinematic mood, warm & moody tones,
high detail, photorealistic rendering

[7] NEGATIVE PROMPT:
no distortion, no extra limbs, no blur on subject,
no artifacts, no overexposure,
no cartoon look, no low-resolution,
no incorrect reflection alignment
```

## AI Restoration of Heavily Damaged Vintage Photo

> Minimal, retro portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
{
  "task": "old_photo_restoration",
  "description": "Restore a heavily damaged vintage photograph of a young girl",
  
  "input_photo_condition": {
    "damage_types": [
      "deep cracks",
      "fold lines",
      "tears across face",
      "scratches",
      "faded colors",
      "paper texture damage",
      "missing parts along crack lines"
    ],
    "age_effects": [
      "yellowish tint",
      "low contrast",
      "washed-out tones",
      "grain/noise"
    ]
  },

  "restoration_goals": {
    "fix_cracks": "remove all cracks and fold marks cleanly",
    "reconstruct_missing_areas": "restore face details naturally where torn",
    "color_correction": "restore natural skin tones without over-saturation",
    "sharpness": "enhance facial details smoothly, maintain softness appropriate for child's portrait",
    "noise_reduction": "remove grain while keeping natural texture",
    "background_cleanup": "smooth restoration of vintage background",
    "preserve_identity": "do NOT alter facial structure or change the person's identity"
  },

  "subject_details": {
    "type": "young girl (approx. 5–7 years old)",
    "hair": "dark brown, two ponytails with small flower hairpins",
    "expression": "smiling, bright eyes",
    "clothing": "vintage floral dress with white collar",
    "pose": "front-facing school-style portrait"
  },

  "output_style": {
    "look": "authentic restored vintage photo",
    "finish": "clean, high-resolution, natural colors",
    "texture": "remove paper wrinkles but preserve slight vintage softness",
    "lighting": "even, soft retro portrait lighting"
  },

  "quality": {
    "resolution": "Ultra HD 4K restoration",
    "clarity": "high but natural (no plastic skin effect)"
  }
}
```

## Hygge Winter Family Photo Prompt

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik manzara üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Create a realistic high-quality winter family photo using the reference faces without changes. 

Scene 

• Snowy winter day in a park or forest with blurred snow-covered trees (bokeh). 

• Soft diffused daylight, cozy warm atmosphere. 

• Neutral palette: beige, cream, white, brown. Bright orange tangerine in mother’s hand as the only accent. 

Mother (Left) 

• Young woman with long wavy hair, smiling at the camera. 

• Brown/taupe faux fur trapper or aviator hat. 

• Matching brown/beige winter coat with faux fur trim. 

• Holding a bright orange tangerine. 

Child (Right, on her lap) 

• About 1 year old, joyful wide smile. 

• White/cream puffy quilted snowsuit. 

• Beige/cream knitted hat with a pom-pom. 

• Light mittens and animal-themed booties (e.g., bear paws). 

Composition & Style 

• Shallow depth of field, strong background blur. 

• Mother sits or half-squats, holding the child closely. 

• Medium shot, centered subjects. 

• Cinematic, hyperrealistic, high resolution, professional post-processing. 

• Cozy hygge winter mood.
```

## Poem-Based Ulzzang Aesthetic Portrait Prompt

> Portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Yazıdan doğrudan üretir, fotoğraf yüklemen gerekmez. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
She wears the breathless noon upon her frame,A silken slice of sky without a name.Below, the faded rhythm of the sea,
In woven denim, casual and free.
A raven cascade falls on shoulders bare,
A shadow contrast to the azure air.
Her face, a porcelain and polished moon,
That glows within the quiet of the room.
The hazel deepness of her open eyes,
Holds secrets of the earth and summer skies.Behind, the soft diffusion of the light,
Turns sanctuary walls to bokeh bright.A living portrait, poised and softly spun,A daughter of the morning and the sun.85mm Portrait Lens, f/1.8 Aperture, Ulzzang Aesthetic, Soft K-Beauty Lighting, Pastel Color Grading, High Fidelity Texture, Rule of Thirds Composition.
```

```
A candid, spontaneous snapshot of a young woman leaning casually out of the window of a vintage car, captured on a bustling European city street at dusk. Warm, natural twilight softly illuminates his relaxed face, highlighting authentic skin texture and the subtle wisp of smoke from his cigarette held loosely in hand. The background shows urban architecture and busy traffic with slight motion blur, emphasizing the car's motion without losing the crisp sharpness of the subject. The composition is casually tilted and framed with genuine spontaneity, evoking the intimate, dynamic feel of authentic iPhone photography in cinematic RAW style. 1:1 aspect ratio
```

## Detailed JSON-based Image Generation Prompt for Gemini Nano Banana Pro

> Portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
{
    "pipeline_sequence_v5": {
      "global_settings": {
        "project_name": "EVENING_FESTIVAL_PORTRAIT",
        "render_target": {
          "width": 1080,
          "height": 1920,
          "ratio": "9:16"
        },
        "seed_strategy": "Fixed",
        "steps": 50
      },

      "node_01_subject_initialization": {
        "module": "Identity_Preservation",
        "parameters": {
          "source_input": "Attached_Image_Buffer",
          "face_restore_strength": 1.0,
          "feature_lock": {
            "eyes": "Strict",
            "nose": "Strict",
            "mouth": "Strict",
            "skin_texture": "Blend_with_Prompt"
          }
        },
        "morphology": {
          "hair": {
            "style": "{argument name="hair style" default="Long_Wavy"}",
            "color": "{argument name="hair color" default="Brown"}",
            "physics": "Natural_Soft"
          },
          "makeup": {
            "lips": "{argument name="lip color" default="Soft_Natural_Pink"}",
            "finish": "Dewy",
            "intensity": "Natural_Glow"
          }
        }
      },

      "node_02_kinematics_control": {
        "module": "ControlNet_OpenPose",
        "pose_skeleton": {
          "body_orientation": "Standing",
          "spine_curve": "Slight_Lean_Back",
          "right_arm": {
            "state": "Relaxed_Down"
          },
          "left_arm": {
            "state": "Relaxed_Down"
          },
          "head_vector": {
            "pitch": 0,
            "yaw": 10,
            "target": "Lens_Direct"
          }
        }
      },

      "node_03_wardrobe_assembly": {
        "module": "Layered_Clothing_Generator",
        "layers": [
          {
            "layer_id": 1,
            "item": "{argument name="clothing item" default="Sleeveless Crop Top"}",
            "pattern": "{argument name="clothing pattern" default="Floral_Oriental"}",
            "color_palette": "{argument name="color palette" default="Navy_Orange_Green"}",
            "fit": "Fitted"
          },
          {
            "layer_id": 2,
            "item": "Accessories",
            "elements": [
              { "type": "Necklace", "style": "Layered_Gold_Choker" },
              { "type": "Earrings", "style": "Small_Drop" }
            ]
          }
        ]
      },

      "node_04_environment_stage": {
        "module": "Scene_Builder",
        "setting_type": "{argument name="setting type" default="Outdoor_Night_Market"}",
        "assets": {
          "lighting_decor": "Hanging_Warm_String_Lights",
          "background_elements": "Plants_Shops_People",
          "ground_material": "Soft_Dirt"
        },
        "atmosphere": {
          "mood": "Warm_Festive_Evening",
          "clutter_density": "Medium_Lively"
        }
      },

      "node_05_lighting_engine": {
        "module": "Illumination",
        "setup": {
          "primary_source": "Warm_Festoon_Lights",
          "fill_light": "Ambient_Night_Soft",
          "color_grading_lut": "Warm_Glow",
          "shadow_quality": "Soft"
        }
      }
    }
  }
```

## Photorealistic East-Asian Neon Night Portrait Prompt

> Fotogerçekçi, sinematik moda çekimi üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Use the provided reference image for the man’s face, hairstyle, and overall appearance → .
Create a high-resolution vertical 4K photorealistic portrait of him standing on a busy East-Asian street at night. His expression is calm but alert, with subtle reflections of neon lights across his skin. He wears a dark hooded jacket layered under a lightweight streetwear coat.

The environment is filled with vibrant neon signage—reds, cyans, and magentas glowing off wet pavement. Light rain falls, visible as streaked droplets caught in headlights and neon reflections. Motion blur from passing cars, pedestrians, and city lights creates a dynamic sense of movement while the subject remains sharply in focus.

Camera: 85mm portrait lens, shallow depth of field, crisp detail on the face (matching the reference image), creamy bokeh in the background. Soft rim-lighting from neon signs edges his silhouette. Wet surfaces reflect saturated colors, enhancing the cinematic mood.

Atmosphere: soft rain mist, drifting steam from a street vent, blurred scooters and cars, neon reflections wrapping around the subject’s shoulders.

Avoid: distorted anatomy, incorrect facial structure (must match reference), double features, overexposure, cartoon/painterly look, text or watermarks, excessive motion blur on the subject.
```

## Hyper-Realistic Two-Panel iPhone Portrait Prompt

> Fotogerçekçi portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
{
  "title": "Hyper-realistic two-panel iPhone portrait — Café (Subject1 + Subject2)",
  "mode": "text_to_image",
  "format": "two_panel_vertical",
  "output_size": "2048x3072",
  "references": {
    "subject1": "subject1_ref.jpg",
    "subject2": "subject2_ref.jpg"
  },
  "prompt": "Hyper-realistic, ultra-photographic two-panel iPhone portrait of two real people, exact-face-matching to the provided reference images (no facial alterations). Both panels share identical angle, spacing, lens perspective and composition so they read as matched diptychs taken in the same seat at the same lush café. Environment: dense bamboo and tropical foliage around a warm wooden table, warm diffused daylight creating gentle highlights and soft, natural shadows across skin, glass, plants and wood. Upper panel — Subject 1: seated, holding a reflective silver phone with a jeweled ring grip catching small bursts of light; deep-red manicured nails; blue-and-white gingham off-shoulder dress with subtle seersucker texture; white lace bandana with delicate perforations; holding a chilled matcha glass with visible condensation droplets, faint fingerprint smudges on the glass, and swirling gradients of matcha and foam at the rim. Lower panel — Subject 2: seated, mirroring the same candid pose, holding a matte metallic phone with tiny scratches and soft reflections; wearing a cream linen shirt with natural slub texture, brown trucker cap (mesh highlights), and beaded accessories; raising a tall iced latte showing layered tones of milk and coffee, melting ice cubes, and misty condensation. Across both panels: micro-details — water rings on the table, smudges on the glass barrier, uneven leaf coloration in foliage, subtle lens glare, micro-reflections on metal and glass, tiny fabric frays and natural skin pores; maintain lifelike texture and micro-imperfections to reinforce authenticity. Lighting: soft, warm, diffused daylight, beautiful skin tones, delicate specular highlights on glass and metal, accurate subsurface scattering on skin. Camera: iPhone portrait-style framing, shallow depth of field with natural bokeh, crisp focal plane on faces and held glasses, matched focal length and angle between panels. Color/tonal: natural, filmic dynamic range, no oversaturation; preserve subtle tonal shifts of matcha and coffee. Composition: two vertically stacked panels, matching framing, consistent perspective and scale so both subjects read as identical-session captures. Strict requirement: faces must match the provided reference images 100% — do not alter facial features, expressions, or identity. Output should be photorealistic and indistinguishable from high-end phone photography.",
  "negative_prompt": "cartoon, painting, CGI, 3D render, anime, stylized, over-saturated, over-contrast, extra limbs, mutated, deformed, blurred, lowres, "
```

## Nostalgic Early 2000s Digital Camera Portrait Prompt

> Retro portre üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Use facial feature of attached photo. A close-up shot of a young woman displayed on the screen of a compact Canon digital camera. The camera body surrounds the image with its buttons, dials, and textured surface visible, including the FUNC/SET wheel, DISP button, and the “IMAGE STABILIZER” label along the side. The photo on the screen shows the woman indoors at night, illuminated by a bright built-in flash that creates sharp highlights on her face and hair. She has long dark hair falling across part of her face in loose strands, with a soft, slightly open-lip expression. The flash accentuates her features against a dim, cluttered kitchen background with appliances, shelves, and metallic surfaces softly blurred. The mood is candid, raw, nostalgic, and reminiscent of early 2000s digital camera snapshots. Colors are slightly muted with cool undertones, strong flash contrast, and natural grain from the display. No text, no logos inside the photo preview itself.

Scale ratio: 4:5 vertical

Camera: compact digital camera simulation
Lens: equivalent to 28–35mm
Aperture: f/2.8
ISO: 400
Shutter speed: 1/60 with flash
White balance: auto flash
Lighting: harsh direct flash on subject, ambient low light in the background
Color grading: nostalgic digital-camera tones, high contrast flash, subtle display grain, authentic screen glow.
```

Soft, natural lighting."

```
Prompt 2:
"Ultra-realistic high-detail indoor portrait of a beautiful woman - keep her real face, natural features, body proportions, and hair EXACTLY the same as the reference (no facial or body changes).
She is wearing a soft {argument name="dress color" default="lavender"} embroidered bodycon dress (updated wearing color), detailed stitching, elegant contours, and a refined luxury-fit silhouette.
Natural soft makeup with smooth skin texture and subtle highlights. Lighting: warm, soft indoor lighting, gentle shadows, natural glow on the face, smooth even complexion, premium portrait depth.
Background: subtle indoor blur (bokeh effect), clean aesthetic, minimal distraction, creamy depth-of-field.
Quality: sharp facial details, natural skin texture, smooth gradients, premium color grading, elegant portrait composition."

Prompt 3:
"Create a photorealistic image of a young woman(match face) sitting at a relaxed outdoor restaurant in {argument name="city" default="London"} on a cool {argument name="day of week" default="Wednesday"} morning in {argument name="month" default="March"} {argument name="year" default="2025"}. The sky is clear, the spring air is crisp, and the city feels calm as it wakes up. She is the focal point of the shot-wearing a light scarf, a black coat and a black turtle neck inside, gently stirring her coffee while gazing off to the side with a thoughtful expression. Everything behind her, from the waiter moving about to the soft morning traffic, should fall into a smooth blur, giving the picture the effortless, candid atmosphere of a moment casually snapped on a phone."

Prompt 4:
"A woman(match face from reference) is holding a cat gently with both hands, looking at it with a soft smile. Her expression is calm, tender, and affectionate. She has long, messy dark brown hair and is wearing a light brown ribbed long-sleeve shirt with buttons. She has small simple earrings, a delicate chain necklace, a thin silver ring on her left hand, and a small botanical tattoo on her right wrist.
The cat is light-colored with cream and beige fur. It sits securely in her arms, looking back at her with a calm, curious expression.
The scene takes place in an indoor room with a plain, neutral-colored wall. Warm sunlight comes through window blinds, casting soft striped shadows across both the woman and the cat.
The overall aesthetic is a soft, natural portrait with a warm, gentle, intimate vibe. The tones include warm beige, soft brown, and natural skin tones. The mood captures a peaceful moment between a human and her pet."
```

```
}
```

## Compact Digital Camera Screen Snapshot of a Young Woman

> Retro görsel üretmek için hazır prompt. Kendi fotoğrafını yükleyerek kullanılır: bu prompt bir REFERANS GÖRSEL ister. Prompt metni İngilizce. Hazır katalog promptu.

```
Use the facial features from the attached photo. A close-up of a young woman is displayed on the screen of a small Canon digital camera. The camera body surrounds the image, with its buttons, dials, and textured surfaces visible, including the FUNC/SET wheel, DISP button, and the 'IMAGE STABILIZER' label on the side. The photo on the screen was taken indoors at night, with the bright built-in flash illuminating the woman's face and hair, creating sharp highlights on her face and strands of hair. Her dark, long hair hangs loosely beside her cheeks, her lips are slightly parted, and her expression is gentle. The flash highlights her facial features, and the background is a dimly lit, cluttered kitchen, where appliances, shelves, and metal surfaces are vaguely visible, showing a soft bokeh effect. The overall atmosphere is natural, authentic, and nostalgic, reminiscent of early 2000s digital camera snapshots. The tone is cool and slightly soft, with the flash creating a strong contrast, and the screen display shows natural grain. No text or logos are present in the photo preview.

Aspect Ratio: 4:5 vertical

Camera: Compact digital camera simulation
Lens: Equivalent 28–35mm
Aperture: f/2.8
ISO: 400
Shutter Speed: 1/60s (using flash)
White Balance: Auto flash
Lighting: Subject is illuminated by strong direct flash, background is weak ambient light
Color Grading Style: Nostalgic digital camera tone, high-contrast flash effect, delicate screen grain, realistic screen glow.
```
